  • 算力用「全部输入 token」算，不是「15% 的目标 token」。

    Compute is counted over all input tokens, not the 15% of target tokens.

  • BERT 的位置表是学出来的（有参数），2017 原版 Transformer 的正余弦位置编码没有参数。

    BERT's position table is learned (it has parameters); the sinusoidal encoding of the 2017 Transformer has none.

BERT 之后：RoBERTa（去掉 NSP、更多数据、动态掩码）、ALBERT（跨层共享权重、分解嵌入）、DeBERTa（改进相对位置）、ModernBERT（8192 上下文、RoPE、Pre-LN）、ViT（同一编码器用在图像块上）。编码器今天主要用于检索向量（RAG）、重排、分类；生成交给了只有解码器的 GPT。

After BERT: RoBERTa (no NSP, more data, dynamic masking), ALBERT (cross-layer weight sharing, factorised embeddings), DeBERTa (improved relative positions), ModernBERT (8192 context, RoPE, Pre-LN), ViT (the same encoder on image patches). Today encoders are mainly used for retrieval embeddings (RAG), reranking and classification; generation has passed to decoder-only GPT models.

← L7 Transformer 与混合专家（MoE）L9 GPT-1、GPT-2、GPT-3：同一个损失，放大 1500 倍 →
LECTURE 09

GPT-1、GPT-2、GPT-3：同一个损失，放大 1500 倍

GPT-1, GPT-2, GPT-3: One Loss, Scaled 1500×

本页目录

一句话：三代 GPT 的架构和损失几乎没变（只有解码器 + 预测下一个词），变的是数据、规模和使用方式：GPT-1 微调 → GPT-2 零样本 → GPT-3 少样本（上下文学习）。

In one sentence: across three GPT generations the architecture and loss barely changed (decoder only + next-word prediction); what changed was the data, the scale and how the model is used: GPT-1 fine-tuning → GPT-2 zero-shot → GPT-3 few-shot (in-context learning).

零基础先读Start here

  • 自回归语言模型：给定前面的词，预测下一个词的概率分布；生成时每次选一个词接在后面，再预测下一个。一句话的概率 = 各个「下一个词」概率的乘积。

    Autoregressive language model: given the previous words, predict a probability distribution over the next word; to generate, pick a word, append it and predict again. A sentence's probability is the product of its next-word probabilities.

  • 为什么一次前向就能训练整句：因果掩码让位置 t 只看到 1…t，所以每个位置同时预测自己的下一个词，一个长 n 的序列一次给出 n 个训练信号（BERT 只有约 15%）。

    Why one forward pass trains a whole sentence: the causal mask lets position t see only 1…t, so every position predicts its own next word at once, and a length-n sequence gives n training signals in one pass (BERT gets only about 15%).

  • 零样本 / 少样本：都不更新权重。零样本只在提示里写任务描述；少样本再放几个例子。微调则要用标注数据更新权重。

    Zero-shot / few-shot: neither updates the weights. Zero-shot puts only a task description in the prompt; few-shot adds a few examples. Fine-tuning, by contrast, updates the weights with labelled data.

  • nat 与 bit：损失用自然对数时单位是 nat；除以 ln 2 换成 bit。

    Nats and bits: a loss in natural logs is in nats; divide by ln 2 to get bits.

  • PF-day：一台每秒 10¹⁵ 次运算的机器跑一天 = 8.64 × 10¹⁹ FLOPs，用来衡量训练算力。

    PF-day: a machine doing 10¹⁵ operations per second running for a day = 8.64 × 10¹⁹ FLOPs; a unit of training compute.

· BERT（L8） GPT（L9、L10）
结构Architecture 只有编码器，双向encoder only, bidirectional 只有解码器，因果掩码decoder only, causal mask
预训练任务Pre-training task MLM（猜被遮的词）+ NSPMLM (guess hidden words) + NSP 预测下一个词next-word prediction
每个序列的训练信号Training signal per sequence 约 15% 的位置about 15% of positions 全部位置all positions
能否生成文本Can it generate text? 不能，n 进 n 出no, n in and n out 能，一次一个词yes, one word at a time
典型用法Typical use 加小头微调做分类、问答、检索fine-tune with a small head for classification, QA, retrieval 微调（GPT-1）、零样本（GPT-2）、少样本（GPT-3）fine-tuning (GPT-1), zero-shot (GPT-2), few-shot (GPT-3)

训练就是一行式子，里面每个符号对应「四个问题」之一：优化器 Update、损失 L、架构 f_θ、数据 D。

Training is one line, and each symbol answers one of the four questions: optimizer Update, loss L, architecture f_θ, data D.

θ←θ−Update(∇θL(fθ(x))),x∼D\theta \leftarrow \theta - \mathrm{Update}\bigl(\nabla_\theta L(f_\theta(x))\bigr), \qquad x \sim Dθ←θ−Update(∇θ​L(fθ​(x))),x∼D

两条必背经验公式Two rules of thumb to memorise

  • 参数量：一个块 = 注意力 4d² + 4d（W_Q、W_K、W_V、W_O 各 d×d 加偏置）+ FFN 8d² + 5d（d→4d→d）+ 两个 LayerNorm 4d = 12d² + 13d。整个模型还要加词嵌入 Vd 和位置嵌入 n_ctx·d（GPT-2/3 再加最后一个 LayerNorm 的 2d）。大模型里块占绝大部分，N ≈ 12Ld²。

    Parameter count: one block = attention 4d² + 4d (W_Q, W_K, W_V, W_O, each d×d plus bias) + FFN 8d² + 5d (d→4d→d) + two LayerNorms 4d = 12d² + 13d. The whole model adds word embeddings Vd and position embeddings n_ctx·d (plus 2d for the final LayerNorm from GPT-2 on). In large models the blocks dominate, so N ≈ 12Ld².

  • FLOPs：一个权重对一个 token 用一次 = 一次乘 + 一次加 = 2 FLOPs。前向 ≈ 2N/token，反向 ≈ 前向的 2 倍，训练 ≈ 6N/token，总训练 ≈ 6ND（D = 处理的 token 数）。1 PF-day = 10¹⁵ × 86,400 = 8.64 × 10¹⁹ FLOPs。

    FLOPs: each use of one weight on one token = one multiply + one add = 2 FLOPs. Forward ≈ 2N per token, backward ≈ twice the forward, training ≈ 6N per token, so total training ≈ 6ND (D = tokens processed). 1 PF-day = 10¹⁵ × 86,400 = 8.64 × 10¹⁹ FLOPs.

  • 2N 规则对嵌入表也对：输入时只是查一行（几乎 0 FLOPs），输出算 logits 时每个权重乘一次（2Vd），刚好算一次。

    The 2N rule also holds for the embedding table: the input side is just a row lookup (almost 0 FLOPs), and computing the output logits uses each weight once (2Vd), so it is counted exactly once.

  • 2N 忽略了注意力里不含参数的计算：QKᵀ 和对 V 的加权各 2nd，合计 4nd FLOPs/token/层。GPT-2 XL 上约多 10%。

    2N ignores attention's parameter-free computation: QKᵀ and the weighting of V cost 2nd each, 4nd FLOPs per token per layer in total — about 10% extra on GPT-2 XL.

N=Vd+nctxd+L (12d2+13d)  (+ 2d)≈12Ld2,Ctrain≈6NDN = Vd + n_{\text{ctx}}d + L\,(12d^2 + 13d) \;(+\,2d) \approx 12Ld^2, \qquad C_{\text{train}} \approx 6NDN=Vd+nctx​d+L(12d2+13d)(+2d)≈12Ld2,Ctrain​≈6ND

GPT-1（2018.6，117M，预训练 + 微调）GPT-1 (June 2018, 117M, pre-training + fine-tuning)

  • 动机：标注数据少、原始文本免费；NLP 还没有像 ImageNet 那样的预训练。三个赌注：① 目标 = 预测下一个词；② 架构 = Transformer 而不是 LSTM（换 LSTM 平均掉 5.6 分）；③ 迁移 = 全部微调，只加一个线性层。左到右的目标只需要左到右的模型，所以是只有解码器。

    Motivation: labelled data is scarce and raw text is free; NLP had no ImageNet-style pre-training yet. Three bets: (1) objective = next-word prediction; (2) architecture = Transformer rather than LSTM (an LSTM loses 5.6 points on average); (3) transfer = fine-tune everything, adding only a linear layer. A left-to-right objective needs only a left-to-right model, hence decoder only.

  • 架构：12 层、d = 768、12 头、FFN 3072、上下文 512、BPE 词表 40,478、GELU、Post-LN、学出来的位置嵌入。因果掩码：位置 i 只能看 ≤ i 的位置，所以一次前向就能在每个位置都得到一个「预测下一个词」的训练信号。

    Architecture: 12 layers, d = 768, 12 heads, FFN 3072, context 512, BPE vocabulary 40,478, GELU, Post-LN, learned position embeddings. Causal mask: position i sees only positions ≤ i, so one forward pass yields a next-word training signal at every position.

  • 权重绑定：输入时 one-hot × W_e（取一行），输出时 h × W_eᵀ（每个词一个分数），只存一份，省 Vd = 31M 参数（GPT-1 的 27%）。词的得分 = 输出向量与该词嵌入的点积。

    Weight tying: the input is one-hot × W_e (a row lookup) and the output is h × W_eᵀ (one score per word), stored once, saving Vd = 31M parameters (27% of GPT-1). A word's score is the dot product of the output vector with that word's embedding.

  • BPE：从单字符开始，反复合并语料里最常见的相邻对（l o w e s t → lo w e s t → low e s t → … → low est）。40,000 次合并 + 478 个初始字符 = 40,478。常见词一个 token，生僻词拆成片段，永远没有「未知词」。模型只看到 token ID。

    BPE: start from single characters and repeatedly merge the most frequent adjacent pair in the corpus (l o w e s t → lo w e s t → low e s t → … → low est). 40,000 merges + 478 initial characters = 40,478. Common words are one token, rare words split into pieces, and there is never an unknown word. The model only sees token IDs.

  • 任务改写成一个序列：分类 ⟨s⟩ 文本 ⟨e⟩；蕴含 ⟨s⟩ 前提 $ 假设 ⟨e⟩；相似度两种顺序都跑、最终状态相加；多选每个选项一个序列、在 k 个分数上 softmax。分类器读最后一个 token ⟨e⟩ 的状态。

    Tasks are rewritten as one sequence: classification ⟨s⟩ text ⟨e⟩; entailment ⟨s⟩ premise $ hypothesis ⟨e⟩; similarity runs both orders and adds the final states; multiple choice uses one sequence per option and a softmax over the k scores. The classifier reads the state of the last token ⟨e⟩.

  • 损失：预训练 L₁ = 下一词对数似然；微调 L₂ = 标签对数似然；合并 L₃ = L₂ + λL₁，λ = 0.5（保留语言模型损失做辅助，泛化更好、收敛更快）。

    Loss: pre-training L₁ = next-word log-likelihood; fine-tuning L₂ = label log-likelihood; combined L₃ = L₂ + λL₁ with λ = 0.5 (keeping the language-model loss as an auxiliary improves generalisation and speeds convergence).

  • 数据：BooksCorpus（7000+ 本未出版书，约 8 亿词），选它是因为有长段连续文本（ELMo 的语料按句打乱、破坏了长程结构）。

    Data: BooksCorpus (7000+ unpublished books, about 800M words), chosen for its long stretches of continuous text (ELMo's corpus was shuffled by sentence, destroying long-range structure).

  • 优化器：Adam、峰值 lr 2.5 × 10⁻⁴、前 2000 步线性 warm-up 再余弦退火到 0、批 64 × 512、100 epoch、L2 0.01、初始化 N(0, 0.02)。微调 lr 6.25 × 10⁻⁵、批 32、约 3 epoch。算力 8 张 GPU 一个月，0.96 PF-day。

    Optimizer: Adam, peak lr 2.5 × 10⁻⁴, linear warm-up over 2000 steps then cosine annealing to 0, batch 64 × 512, 100 epochs, L2 0.01, initialisation N(0, 0.02). Fine-tuning: lr 6.25 × 10⁻⁵, batch 32, about 3 epochs. Compute: 8 GPUs for a month, 0.96 PF-day.

  • 结果：12 个任务里 9 个 SOTA，GLUE 72.8；四个月后 BERT 以 80.5 大幅超越。OpenAI 没有转向 BERT，而是坚持下一词预测。

    Results: state of the art on 9 of 12 tasks, GLUE 72.8; four months later BERT beat it decisively with 80.5. OpenAI did not switch to BERT and stuck with next-word prediction.

L=−1T∑t=1Tlog⁡pθ(ut+1∣u≤t),pθ=softmax(htWe⊤),L3=L2+λL1L = -\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log p_\theta(u_{t+1} \mid u_{\le t}), \qquad p_\theta = \mathrm{softmax}(h_t W_e^{\top}), \qquad L_3 = L_2 + \lambda L_1L=−T1​t=1∑T​logpθ​(ut+1​∣u≤t​),pθ​=softmax(ht​We⊤​),L3​=L2​+λL1​

困惑度（perplexity）：PPL = e^L。PPL = k 的意思是：模型平均像在 k 个等可能的选项里猜。公平骰子 PPL = 6；完全不会（均匀）PPL = V；GPT-1 预训练后 18.4。它不是错误率，只是把损失换成「选项个数」。实用习惯：初始化时的损失应该 ≈ ln V（GPT-1：ln 40,478 = 10.61），不是就是初始化或数据管道有问题。另一个单位：bits/token = L / ln 2。

Perplexity: PPL = e^L. PPL = k means the model is, on average, guessing among k equally likely options. A fair die has PPL 6; a clueless (uniform) model has PPL V; GPT-1 after pre-training reaches 18.4. It is not an error rate, just the loss restated as a number of options. Practical habit: the loss at initialisation should be ≈ ln V (GPT-1: ln 40,478 = 10.61); if not, the initialisation or data pipeline is broken. Another unit: bits/token = L / ln 2.

推理面试题（前向传播次数 FP、KV 缓存）Inference interview questions (forward passes, KV cache)

  • 输入 30 token、输出 1000 token：1 次 prefill（给出第 1 个输出）+ 999 次解码 + 1 次生成 EOS = 1001。只要上下文没满，模型只有生成 EOS 才会停。

    30 input tokens, 1000 output tokens: 1 prefill (giving the 1st output) + 999 decoding steps + 1 step to generate EOS = 1001. Unless the context fills up, the model stops only by generating EOS.

  • 多轮对话：每轮都是 1 次 prefill（新输入，历史在 KV 缓存里）+ (输出 − 1) + 1。例：输出 100/200/300 → 101 + 201 + 301 = 603。

    Multi-turn chat: each turn is 1 prefill (the new input; history is in the KV cache) + (output − 1) + 1. Example: outputs of 100/200/300 → 101 + 201 + 301 = 603.

  • prefill 并不会算 1000 个 logits（只需要最后一个）；但 1000 个 token 的 KV 缓存可以并行算——token 间并行，层间串行。

    Prefill does not compute 1000 sets of logits (only the last is needed), but the KV cache for 1000 tokens can be computed in parallel — parallel across tokens, sequential across layers.

  • BERT 是编码器：30 进 30 出，1 次 FP；它不能生成比输入长的输出。

    BERT is an encoder: 30 in, 30 out, 1 forward pass; it cannot produce output longer than its input.

  • Qwen3-VL：视觉编码器是 ViT（像 BERT）。图像输入 30 出 30：1（编码器）+ 1（MLP merger）+ 31（解码器）= 33；文本输入直接进解码器 = 31。

    Qwen3-VL: the vision encoder is a ViT (BERT-like). Image input, 30 out: 1 (encoder) + 1 (MLP merger) + 31 (decoder) = 33; text input goes straight to the decoder = 31.

  • 预测第 K 个输出时只存前 N + K − 1 个 token 的 KV，因为后面的 token 还没生成。

    When predicting the K-th output, only the KV of the first N + K − 1 tokens is stored, because later tokens do not exist yet.

GPT-2（2019.2，1.5B，零样本）GPT-2 (February 2019, 1.5B, zero-shot)

  • 动机：微调得到的是「窄专家」，每个任务要标注、要重新训练、分布一变就脆弱。问题：不给例子、不更新权重，语言模型能不能做没训练过的任务？思路：用语言本身描述任务，p(输出 ∣ 输入, 任务)。

    Motivation: fine-tuning produces narrow experts — every task needs labels and retraining, and they break when the distribution shifts. Question: with no examples and no weight updates, can a language model do tasks it was never trained on? Idea: describe the task in language itself, p(output ∣ input, task).

  • 架构（只有小改）：Pre-LN（LayerNorm 移到子层输入，残差通路干净，深层更稳）；最后一块后再加一个 LayerNorm；残差层权重初始化乘 1/√N_res；上下文 1024；字节级 BPE 词表 50,257。四个尺寸：124M（12 层，768）、355M（24，1024）、774M（36，1280）、1,558M（48，1600）。

    Architecture (small changes only): Pre-LN (LayerNorm moved to each sublayer's input, leaving a clean residual path, more stable when deep); an extra LayerNorm after the last block; residual-layer weights initialised scaled by 1/√N_res; context 1024; byte-level BPE vocabulary of 50,257. Four sizes: 124M (12 layers, 768), 355M (24, 1024), 774M (36, 1280), 1,558M (48, 1600).

  • 损失：同样的下一词交叉熵，去掉微调损失。生成用 top-k 采样（k = 40）：只保留概率最高的 k 个，重新归一化后采样。用法：把任务写进提示（文章 + 「TL;DR:」→ 摘要；「英文 = 法文」对 → 翻译）。

    Loss: the same next-word cross-entropy, with the fine-tuning loss dropped. Generation uses top-k sampling (k = 40): keep only the k most likely words, renormalise and sample. Usage: write the task into the prompt (article + "TL;DR:" → summary; "English = French" pairs → translation).

  • 数据：WebText——Reddit 上 karma ≥ 3 的外链，4500 万链接 → 去重后 800 万文档、约 40 GB；去掉维基百科（避免与测试集重叠）。零样本要求训练文本本身就含有各种任务，小说没有，网页有。

    Data: WebText — outbound links from Reddit posts with karma ≥ 3, 45M links → 8M documents after deduplication, about 40 GB; Wikipedia removed (to avoid overlap with test sets). Zero-shot needs training text that already contains all kinds of tasks; novels do not, web pages do.

  • 优化器：只知道批 512 × 1024、学习率手动调。关键句子「所有模型仍然欠拟合 WebText」→ 曲线还没平，更大的模型会更好，这就是 GPT-3 的动机。

    Optimizer: all we know is batch 512 × 1024 and a hand-tuned learning rate. The key sentence, "all models still underfit WebText", means the curves have not flattened and bigger models will do better — the motivation for GPT-3.

  • 结果：8 个语言建模基准里 7 个 SOTA；CoQA 零样本 55 F1；翻译摘要能做但很弱（英→法 5 BLEU）；性能随模型大小对数线性提升。分阶段发布。

    Results: state of the art on 7 of 8 language-modelling benchmarks; CoQA zero-shot 55 F1; translation and summarisation work but weakly (English→French 5 BLEU); performance improves log-linearly with model size. Released in stages.

GPT-3（2020.5，175B，少样本 / 上下文学习）GPT-3 (May 2020, 175B, few-shot / in-context learning)

  • 动机：GPT-2 零样本与微调模型的差距，是思路错了，还是只是规模不够？依据：GPT-2 的对数线性曲线、Scaling Laws（测试损失随参数/数据/算力呈幂律下降）、「The Bitter Lesson」、微软的 10 亿美元。决定：不改架构、不改损失，把 GPT-2 放大 100 倍。

    Motivation: was the gap between GPT-2 zero-shot and fine-tuned models a wrong idea or just too little scale? Evidence: GPT-2's log-linear curves, the Scaling Laws (test loss falls as a power law in parameters, data and compute), "The Bitter Lesson", and Microsoft's $1 billion. Decision: change neither architecture nor loss, and scale GPT-2 up 100×.

  • 上下文学习：零样本 = 只有任务描述；单样本 = 加 1 个例子；少样本 = 加 K 个例子（常见 10–100，放得进 2048 token 为止）。三种都不更新权重，「学习」发生在一次前向里。外循环：预训练 SGD 学到很多技能；内循环：前向识别提示要的是哪个技能。任务从权重里搬到了输入里。

    In-context learning: zero-shot = task description only; one-shot = plus 1 example; few-shot = plus K examples (usually 10–100, as many as fit in 2048 tokens). None of the three updates the weights; the "learning" happens within one forward pass. Outer loop: pre-training SGD learns many skills; inner loop: the forward pass recognises which skill the prompt asks for. The task has moved from the weights into the input.

  • 架构：GPT-2 放大，96 层、d = 12,288、96 头、d_head = 128、上下文 2048；唯一变化是注意力在稠密和局部带状稀疏之间交替。训了 8 个尺寸（125M 到 175B）来测规模效应。

    Architecture: GPT-2 scaled up — 96 layers, d = 12,288, 96 heads, d_head = 128, context 2048; the only change is that attention alternates between dense and locally banded sparse patterns. Eight sizes (125M to 175B) were trained to measure scaling effects.

  • 损失：还是下一词交叉熵。新的是评分方式：多选题用平均每 token 对数似然（公平对待长答案）；自由回答用 beam search（宽 4、长度惩罚 0.6）。

    Loss: still next-word cross-entropy. What is new is scoring: multiple choice uses the average log-likelihood per token (fair to long answers); free-form answers use beam search (width 4, length penalty 0.6).

  • 数据：过滤后的 Common Crawl 410B（权重 60%）、WebText2 19B（22%）、Books1 12B（8%）、Books2 55B（8%）、维基 3B（3%）；共 499B，实际训练 300B。按质量采样而不是按大小（WebText2 只占 4% 的 token 却占每批 22%）。不到一个 epoch——先用完的是算力。去污染过滤器有 bug，但重训太贵。

    Data: filtered Common Crawl 410B (weight 60%), WebText2 19B (22%), Books1 12B (8%), Books2 55B (8%), Wikipedia 3B (3%); 499B in total, 300B actually trained on. Sampling follows quality, not size (WebText2 is only 4% of the tokens but 22% of each batch). Less than one epoch — compute ran out first. The decontamination filter had a bug, but retraining was too expensive.

  • 优化器：Adam（β₁ = 0.9、β₂ = 0.95、ε = 10⁻⁸）、梯度裁剪全局范数 1.0、lr 0.6 × 10⁻⁴、前 375M token warm-up、余弦衰减到峰值 10%（260B token）后保持、权重衰减 0.1、批 3.2M token（从 32K 逐渐增大）。规律：模型越大，批越大、学习率越小（125M：0.5M / 6 × 10⁻⁴；175B：3.2M / 0.6 × 10⁻⁴）。

    Optimizer: Adam (β₁ = 0.9, β₂ = 0.95, ε = 10⁻⁸), gradient clipping at global norm 1.0, lr 0.6 × 10⁻⁴, warm-up over the first 375M tokens, cosine decay to 10% of peak (by 260B tokens) and then held, weight decay 0.1, batch 3.2M tokens (ramped up from 32K). Pattern: the bigger the model, the larger the batch and the smaller the learning rate (125M: 0.5M / 6 × 10⁻⁴; 175B: 3.2M / 0.6 × 10⁻⁴).

  • 结果：CoQA 零样本 81.5、少样本 85.0；TriviaQA 71.2%；人类分辨生成新闻仅 52%。主要结论是一条曲线：规模越大，少样本提升越多。仅 API 提供，权重不公开。

    Results: CoQA zero-shot 81.5, few-shot 85.0; TriviaQA 71.2%; humans identify generated news only 52% of the time. The main finding is one curve: the larger the model, the more few-shot helps. Offered only through an API; weights not released.

  • 部分作者随后离开创立 Anthropic；「怎么在这个规模上训练」的知识主要在人身上，不在论文里。

    Some of the authors later left to found Anthropic; the know-how of training at this scale lives mainly in people, not in papers.

  • GPT-3 少样本不是倒退：同一模型也测了零样本（且远超 GPT-2）；GPT-2 的零样本可能是在重复训练数据里的模式；少样本可以用生造词（farduddle）测试，更可信。ChatGPT 则把例子从提示搬回权重（指令微调 + RLHF），最终实现实用的零样本。

    GPT-3's few-shot is not a step backwards: the same model was also tested zero-shot (far above GPT-2); GPT-2's zero-shot may have been repeating patterns from its training data; few-shot can be tested with made-up words (farduddle), which is more convincing. ChatGPT then moved the examples from the prompt back into the weights (instruction tuning + RLHF), finally giving practical zero-shot.

score(a)=1∣a∣∑j=1∣a∣log⁡pθ(aj∣prompt,a<j)\mathrm{score}(a) = \frac{1}{|a|}\sum_{j=1}^{|a|}\log p_\theta\bigl(a_j \mid \text{prompt}, a_{<j}\bigr)score(a)=∣a∣1​j=1∑∣a∣​logpθ​(aj​∣prompt,a<j​)

算力Compute 0.96 PF-day 未报告not reported 3,640 PF-day
用法Usage 每个任务微调fine-tune per task 零样本提示zero-shot prompting 少样本提示few-shot prompting

课堂习题答案要点Key points from the class exercises

  • GPT-1 FLOPs：N = 116.5M；512 token 前向 2N × 512 = 1.19 × 10¹¹；每步 6N × 32,768 = 2.29 × 10¹³；100 epoch × 10⁹ token → 6ND = 7.0 × 10¹⁹ ≈ 0.81 PF-day；硬件估算 8 × 12T × 33% × 30 天 ≈ 0.95 PF-day，与官方 0.96 吻合。

    GPT-1 FLOPs: N = 116.5M; a 512-token forward pass costs 2N × 512 = 1.19 × 10¹¹; one step 6N × 32,768 = 2.29 × 10¹³; 100 epochs × 10⁹ tokens → 6ND = 7.0 × 10¹⁹ ≈ 0.81 PF-day; a hardware estimate of 8 × 12T × 33% × 30 days ≈ 0.95 PF-day matches the official 0.96.

  • 下一词损失：logits (1, 0, 2, 0.5)、真词 sat → p = 0.579、L = 0.546；概率 1/2、1/4、1/8 → 平均 2 ln 2 = 1.386、PPL = 4；初始 ln 40,478 = 10.61，PPL 18.4 对应 L = 2.91。

    Next-word loss: logits (1, 0, 2, 0.5) with true word sat → p = 0.579, L = 0.546; probabilities 1/2, 1/4, 1/8 → average 2 ln 2 = 1.386, PPL = 4; at initialisation ln 40,478 = 10.61, and PPL 18.4 corresponds to L = 2.91.

  • GPT-2 参数：Small 124,439,808、XL 1,557,611,200（论文里的 117M/1542M 是错的）；嵌入占比 Small 31.6%、XL 5.3%——嵌入随 d 增长，块随 Ld² 增长。

    GPT-2 parameters: Small 124,439,808, XL 1,557,611,200 (the paper's 117M/1542M are wrong); embeddings are 31.6% of Small and 5.3% of XL — embeddings grow with d, blocks with Ld².

  • GPT-2 XL FLOPs：2N = 3.12 × 10⁹/token，注意力额外 4nd·L = 3.15 × 10⁸（约 10%）；每步 4.9 PF-秒；一个 epoch（10¹⁰ token）≈ 1.08 PF-day，已超过 GPT-1 整个训练。

    GPT-2 XL FLOPs: 2N = 3.12 × 10⁹ per token, plus 4nd·L = 3.15 × 10⁸ for attention (about 10%); 4.9 PF-seconds per step; one epoch (10¹⁰ tokens) ≈ 1.08 PF-day, already more than all of GPT-1's training.

  • 权重绑定 + top-k：logit = e_w·h。三个位置损失 0.412、0.820、0.494，平均 0.575、PPL 1.78、0.83 bits；top-2 保留 C、A，重归一化为 0.731、0.269。h₂ 与 e_B、e_C 同等对齐，至少付 ln 2。

    Weight tying + top-k: logit = e_w·h. Losses at the three positions are 0.412, 0.820, 0.494, average 0.575, PPL 1.78, 0.83 bits; top-2 keeps C and A, renormalised to 0.731 and 0.269. h₂ is equally aligned with e_B and e_C, so it pays at least ln 2.

  • GPT-3 参数与显存：每块 1,812,099,072，总计 174.6B（12Ld² 占 99.6%，嵌入仅 0.37%）；fp16 349 GB（至少 11 张 32 GB V100）、fp32 698 GB；加 Adam 的 m、v 约 2.1 TB。

    GPT-3 parameters and memory: 1,812,099,072 per block, 174.6B in total (12Ld² is 99.6%, embeddings only 0.37%); 349 GB in fp16 (at least 11 V100s with 32 GB), 698 GB in fp32; adding Adam's m and v gives about 2.1 TB.

  • GPT-3 FLOPs：6ND = 3.14 × 10²³ = 3,640 PF-day；93,750 步；约 356 V100-年。推理 2000 token 提示 + 生成 48 个：无 KV 缓存 3.4 × 10¹⁶，有缓存 7.1 × 10¹⁴（省 47 倍）。少样本每次查询都要重读例子，微调只付一次。

    GPT-3 FLOPs: 6ND = 3.14 × 10²³ = 3,640 PF-days; 93,750 steps; about 356 V100-years. Inference with a 2000-token prompt and 48 generated tokens: 3.4 × 10¹⁶ without a KV cache, 7.1 × 10¹⁴ with one (47× less). Few-shot re-reads the examples on every query; fine-tuning pays once.

  • 总概率 vs 每 token 平均：A (0.30)、B (0.60, 0.45)、C (0.50, 0.50, 0.90)：总概率选 A，每 token 平均选 C——乘积随长度变小，偏向短答案；除以长度去掉这个偏差。

    Total probability vs per-token average: A (0.30), B (0.60, 0.45), C (0.50, 0.50, 0.90): total probability picks A, the per-token average picks C — the product shrinks with length and favours short answers; dividing by length removes that bias.

  • Adam 第一步：z = (2, 1, 0, 0)、真词第 3 个：L = 2.494、g = p − y。偏差修正后 m̂ = g、v̂ = g²，步长 = −η·sign(g)，每个坐标都移动 η，新损失 1.627；SGD 同学习率只到 1.885。这就是大模型要 warm-up、小学习率、梯度裁剪的原因。

    Adam's first step: z = (2, 1, 0, 0) with the 3rd word true: L = 2.494, g = p − y. After bias correction m̂ = g and v̂ = g², so the step is −η·sign(g): every coordinate moves by η, and the new loss is 1.627; SGD with the same learning rate only reaches 1.885. This is why large models need warm-up, small learning rates and gradient clipping.

m1=(1−β1)g,  v1=(1−β2)g2,  m^=m11−β1=g,  v^=v11−β2=g2  ⇒  Δθ=−η m^v^=−η sign(g)m_1 = (1-\beta_1)g,\; v_1 = (1-\beta_2)g^2,\; \hat m = \frac{m_1}{1-\beta_1} = g,\; \hat v = \frac{v_1}{1-\beta_2} = g^2 \;\Rightarrow\; \Delta\theta = -\eta\,\frac{\hat m}{\sqrt{\hat v}} = -\eta\,\mathrm{sign}(g)m1​=(1−β1​)g,v1​=(1−β2​)g2,m^=1−β1​m1​​=g,v^=1−β2​v1​​=g2⇒Δθ=−ηv^​m^​=−ηsign(g)

Quiz 9 答案与关键步骤Quiz 9 answers and key steps

点开看答案与步骤 · Show answers and steps

  • Q1 升级后新增的参数（12 块、d = 384、FFN 中间宽度 1536 → 1920、词表 +256、位置表 +512 行、最后加一个 LN、嵌入绑定、每参数 2 字节）：

    Q1 parameters added by an upgrade (12 blocks, d = 384, FFN hidden width 1536 → 1920, vocabulary +256, position table +512 rows, one final LN added, tied embeddings, 2 bytes per parameter):

    • 每块 FFN 多出 384 个中间单元：第一层权重 384 × 384 = 147,456，偏置 +384；第二层权重 384 × 384 = 147,456，偏置不变（输出仍是 384 维）。每块 295,296，12 块 3,543,552。

      Each block's FFN gains 384 hidden units: first-layer weights 384 × 384 = 147,456 plus 384 biases; second-layer weights 384 × 384 = 147,456 with no new biases (the output is still 384-dimensional). 295,296 per block, 3,543,552 for 12 blocks.

    • 词嵌入 256 × 384 = 98,304（绑定，只算一次）；位置 512 × 384 = 196,608；最后 LN 2 × 384 = 768。

      Word embeddings 256 × 384 = 98,304 (tied, counted once); positions 512 × 384 = 196,608; final LN 2 × 384 = 768.

    • 合计 3,839,232 个，× 2 字节 ÷ 2²⁰ ≈ 7.32 MiB。

      Total 3,839,232 parameters; × 2 bytes ÷ 2²⁰ ≈ 7.32 MiB.

  • Q2 换 batch 后的 warm-up（N = 8×10⁷，原来 64 × 512 token/步、warm-up 256 步，现在 128 × 512）：

    Q2 warm-up after changing the batch (N = 8×10⁷; previously 64 × 512 tokens per step with 256 warm-up steps, now 128 × 512):

    • warm-up 要处理的 token 数不变：256 × 64 × 512 = 8,388,608；新的每步 65,536 → S = 128 步。

      The number of warm-up tokens stays the same: 256 × 64 × 512 = 8,388,608; at 65,536 per step that is S = 128 steps.

    • 第 48 步后：48 × 65,536 = 3,145,728 token；η(48) = 2.4×10⁻⁴ × 48/128 = 9×10⁻⁵；算力 6 × 8×10⁷ × 3,145,728 ≈ 1.51×10¹⁵ FLOPs。

      After step 48: 48 × 65,536 = 3,145,728 tokens; η(48) = 2.4×10⁻⁴ × 48/128 = 9×10⁻⁵; compute 6 × 8×10⁷ × 3,145,728 ≈ 1.51×10¹⁵ FLOPs.

  • Q3 KV 缓存省了多少（32 token 的提示已 prefill，只算接下来 8 轮解码）：

    Q3 how much the KV cache saves (a 32-token prompt already prefilled; count only the next 8 decoding steps):

    • 不缓存：每轮重算整个序列的 K、V，长度 33, 34, …, 40，共 (33 + 40) × 8/2 = 292 个 token 位置。

      Without a cache: each step recomputes K and V for the whole sequence of length 33, 34, …, 40, a total of (33 + 40) × 8/2 = 292 token positions.

    • 缓存：每轮只算新 token，共 8 个。

      With a cache: only the new token each step, 8 in total.

    • 减少 (292 − 8)/292 ≈ 97.3%。

      Reduction: (292 − 8)/292 ≈ 97.3%.

  • Q4 不同分词器怎么比较（20 字节的同一段文本）：

    Q4 comparing different tokenisers (the same 20-byte text):

    • A 切成 8 个 token，4 个概率 1/2、4 个 1/4：总 NLL = 4 ln 2 + 8 ln 2 = 12 ln 2；平均 1.5 ln 2；PPL = 2^1.5 ≈ 2.83；bits/byte = 12 ln 2/(20 ln 2) = 0.600。

      A gives 8 tokens, 4 with probability 1/2 and 4 with 1/4: total NLL = 4 ln 2 + 8 ln 2 = 12 ln 2; average 1.5 ln 2; PPL = 2^1.5 ≈ 2.83; bits/byte = 12 ln 2/(20 ln 2) = 0.600.

    • B 切成 3 个 token，概率 1/8、1/8、1/16：总 NLL = (3 + 3 + 4) ln 2 = 10 ln 2；平均 (10/3) ln 2；PPL = 2^(10/3) ≈ 10.1；bits/byte = 10/20 = 0.500。

      B gives 3 tokens with probabilities 1/8, 1/8, 1/16: total NLL = (3 + 3 + 4) ln 2 = 10 ln 2; average (10/3) ln 2; PPL = 2^(10/3) ≈ 10.1; bits/byte = 10/20 = 0.500.

    • 结论：PPL 说 A 好，bits/byte 说 B 好，排序相反。不同分词器的 token 不一样大，PPL 不能跨分词器比较，要用按字节归一的 bits/byte。

      Conclusion: PPL favours A, bits/byte favours B — opposite rankings. Different tokenisers produce tokens of different sizes, so PPL cannot be compared across tokenisers; use byte-normalised bits/byte.

  • Q5 共享偏置的校准（z₁ = (θ, 0) 目标 A；z₂ = (θ, ln 4) 目标 B）：

    Q5 calibrating a shared bias (z₁ = (θ, 0) with target A; z₂ = (θ, ln 4) with target B):

    • p_A(1) = e^θ/(e^θ + 1)，p_B(2) = 4/(e^θ + 4)。

      p_A(1) = e^θ/(e^θ + 1) and p_B(2) = 4/(e^θ + 4).

    • 平均损失对 θ 求导并令其为 0：1/(e^θ + 1) = e^θ/(e^θ + 4) ⇒ e^(2θ) = 4 ⇒ θ = ln 2。

      Set the derivative of the average loss with respect to θ to 0: 1/(e^θ + 1) = e^θ/(e^θ + 4) ⇒ e^(2θ) = 4 ⇒ θ = ln 2.

    • 此时两个正确概率都是 2/3，平均损失 ln 1.5 ≈ 0.405。交叉熵对 logit 是凸的（二阶导数 > 0），所以是最小值。

      Both correct-class probabilities are then 2/3 and the average loss is ln 1.5 ≈ 0.405. Cross-entropy is convex in the logits (second derivative > 0), so this is the minimum.

  • Q6 top-k 的覆盖率（p = (0.32, 0.24, 0.18, 0.12, 0.09, 0.05)，C、D 是关键词，独立抽 3 次，至少一次抽到关键词的概率 ≥ 70%）：

    Q6 coverage under top-k (p = (0.32, 0.24, 0.18, 0.12, 0.09, 0.05), C and D are the key words, 3 independent draws, need at least a 70% chance of drawing a key word at least once):

    • k = 1、2：C、D 被切掉，概率 0。

      k = 1 or 2: C and D are cut off, probability 0.

    • k = 3：保留和 0.74，P(C) = 0.18/0.74 ≈ 0.243；1 − (1 − 0.243)³ ≈ 56.7%，不够。

      k = 3: kept mass 0.74, P(C) = 0.18/0.74 ≈ 0.243; 1 − (1 − 0.243)³ ≈ 56.7%, not enough.

    • k = 4：保留和 0.86，P(C 或 D) = 0.30/0.86 ≈ 0.349；1 − (0.651)³ ≈ 72.4%，达标。答案 k = 4。

      k = 4: kept mass 0.86, P(C or D) = 0.30/0.86 ≈ 0.349; 1 − (0.651)³ ≈ 72.4%, which meets the target. Answer: k = 4.

L9 易错点L9 common mistakes

  • 参数公式里嵌入绑定时只算一次；GPT-2 起要加最后一个 LayerNorm 的 2d；位置表是学出来的，有 n_ctx·d 个参数。

    In the parameter formula, tied embeddings count once; from GPT-2 on add 2d for the final LayerNorm; the position table is learned and has n_ctx·d parameters.

  • 前向 2N、训练 6N 是「每个 token」；总量要乘 token 数，不是样本数。

    Forward 2N and training 6N are per token; totals multiply by the number of tokens, not samples.

  • PPL = e^(平均损失)，不是 e^(总损失)；bits = nats / ln 2。

    PPL = e^(average loss), not e^(total loss); bits = nats / ln 2.

  • 初始化时损失应该接近 ln V。

    At initialisation the loss should be close to ln V.

  • 生成时的前向次数 = 1（prefill）+ 输出长度 − 1 + 1（EOS）；多轮对话每轮都重新 prefill 新输入。

    Forward passes in generation = 1 (prefill) + output length − 1 + 1 (EOS); in multi-turn chat every turn prefills its new input.

  • BERT 不能生成：输入 n 个 token 就输出 n 个向量，一次前向。

    BERT cannot generate: n tokens in, n vectors out, in one forward pass.

  • Adam 第一步的移动量是 η·sign(g)，和梯度大小无关。

    Adam's first step moves by η·sign(g), regardless of the gradient's size.

  • GPT-3 的 β₂ = 0.95（不是默认的 0.999）。

    GPT-3 uses β₂ = 0.95 (not the default 0.999).

LECTURE 10

GPT 推理：prefill/decode、KV 缓存与解码策略

GPT Inference: Prefill/Decode, KV Cache and Decoding Strategies

本页目录

说明：L10 课件是 L9（GPT-1/2/3）的扩充版，前面内容相同，这里只记新增部分：推理的两个阶段、KV 缓存的细节、以及「怎么从概率里选出一个词」。

Note: the L10 slides extend L9 (GPT-1/2/3) and repeat its first part, so only the new material is covered here: the two phases of inference, KV-cache details, and how one word is chosen from the probabilities.

一句话：模型只给出概率；「选哪个词」是推理时另外的规则，不改权重、不进损失。同一个训好的模型可以搭配任意解码规则。

In one sentence: the model only outputs probabilities; which word to pick is a separate rule applied at inference, changing no weights and never entering the loss. The same trained model can be paired with any decoding rule.

零基础先读：训练和推理的区别Start here: training vs inference

  • 训练：答案已知，只问「你给正确答案多少概率」，算损失、反向传播、更新权重；什么都不「选」。

    Training: the answer is known; we only ask how much probability the model gives it, compute the loss, backpropagate and update the weights. Nothing is chosen.

  • 推理（生成）：答案未知，模型给出下一个词的概率，另外一个规则选出一个词给用户看；不更新任何权重。

    Inference (generation): the answer is unknown; the model gives next-word probabilities and a separate rule picks one word to show the user. No weights are updated.

  • EOS：序列结束符。模型生成 EOS 才停（或写满上下文）。

    EOS: the end-of-sequence token. The model stops when it generates EOS (or when the context is full).

  • 前向传播次数公式：一轮对话 = 1（prefill，同时给出第 1 个输出词）+ (输出词数 − 1) + 1（生成 EOS）= 输出词数 + 1。

    Forward-pass formula: one turn = 1 (prefill, which also gives the 1st output word) + (output words − 1) + 1 (generating EOS) = output words + 1.

  • 一个解码步的全过程：最后一层的隐藏向量（1×d）→ 乘 W_eᵀ 得到 V 个 logits → softmax 得到概率 → 选一个词 → 只把这个词的 ID（一个整数）和 KV 缓存交给下一步。GPT-3：d = 12,288，V = 50,257。

    One decoding step end to end: last-layer hidden vector (1×d) → multiply by W_eᵀ to get V logits → softmax to probabilities → pick one word → pass only that word's ID (one integer) and the KV cache to the next step. GPT-3: d = 12,288, V = 50,257.

推理的两个阶段The two phases of inference

  • Prefill（预填充）：一次读入整个提示。所有提示 token 事先已知，而每个 token 的 Kᵢ = W_K xᵢ、Vᵢ = W_V xᵢ 只依赖它自己，所以并行算出整个 KV 缓存（token 间并行，层与层之间仍是串行）。同时给出答案的第一个词。

    Prefill: read the whole prompt at once. All prompt tokens are known in advance, and each token's Kᵢ = W_K xᵢ and Vᵢ = W_V xᵢ depend only on itself, so the whole KV cache is computed in parallel (parallel across tokens, still sequential across layers). It also produces the first word of the answer.

  • Decode（解码）：每次只输入最新的一个 token，算它的 q、k、v，把 k、v 追加到缓存，用 q 去看缓存里所有 k、v。必须串行：下一个 token 就是从当前 token 预测出来的，预测之前它还不存在。

    Decode: feed in only the newest token, compute its q, k, v, append k and v to the cache, and use q to attend over all cached k and v. This must be sequential: the next token is predicted from the current one and does not exist before that.

  • 用户感受：prefill 决定「第一个字出来前等多久」，decode 决定「字出来得多快」。

    What the user feels: prefill sets how long until the first word appears; decode sets how fast words keep coming.

  • 例：「What is the capital of France ?」7 个 token → 答「The capital of France is Paris .」：1 次 prefill（同时产出 The）+ 6 次 decode + 1 次产出 EOS = 8 次前向。

    Example: "What is the capital of France ?" (7 tokens) → answer "The capital of France is Paris .": 1 prefill (which also outputs The) + 6 decodes + 1 to output EOS = 8 forward passes.

· Prefill Decode
输入Input 整个提示the whole prompt 只有最新的 tokenonly the newest token
前向次数Forward passes 1 次，不管提示多长1, however long the prompt 每个新 token 1 次1 per new token
token 间并行？Parallel across tokens? 是，全部已知yes, all are known 否，每个需要前一个no, each needs the previous one
输出Output KV 缓存 + 第一个输出 tokenKV cache + first output token 下一个 token；缓存长 1next token; cache grows by 1

小测：要算几次全词表 softmax？（1000 个 token）Quick check: how many full-vocabulary softmaxes? (1000 tokens)

  • Prefill：1 次。位置 1–999 的下一个词已在提示里，它们只用来填 KV 缓存；只有第 1000 个位置要预测未知的下一个词。

    Prefill: once. The next words for positions 1–999 are already in the prompt, so those positions only fill the KV cache; only position 1000 predicts an unknown next word.

  • 训练：1000 次。每个位置都要和真实下一个词比较，损失是 1000 项的平均。

    Training: 1000 times. Every position is compared with its true next word, and the loss averages 1000 terms.

  • 记忆：训练在每个位置问「你对了吗」，推理只问一次「下一个是什么」。

    To remember: training asks "were you right?" at every position; inference asks "what comes next?" once.

KV 缓存：存什么、不存什么KV cache: what is stored and what is not

  • 新 token（第 5 个）的注意力只用一个 query q₅ 去看 k₁…k₅ 和 v₁…v₅；q₁…q₄ 根本不出现。

    The new token's (the 5th) attention uses a single query q₅ against k₁…k₅ and v₁…v₅; q₁…q₄ never appear.

  • 为什么没有 Q 缓存：query 是当前 token 的提问（「哪些 token 对我重要？」），只用一次就扔；key 和 value 是每个 token 提供的答案，后面每个 token 都要反复读，所以要存。

    Why there is no Q cache: a query is the current token's question ("which tokens matter to me?"), used once and discarded; keys and values are the answers each token offers, read again by every later token, so they are stored.

  • 一步交给下一步的只有两样：① 选中的 token ID（一个整数，下一步用它取 W_e 的一行）；② KV 缓存。logits、概率、输出向量、q 都不传。

    Only two things pass from one step to the next: (1) the chosen token ID (one integer, used to fetch a row of W_e); (2) the KV cache. Logits, probabilities, output vectors and q are not passed on.

αj=q5⋅kjdk,α^=softmax(α),out5=∑j=15α^jvj\alpha_j = \frac{q_5 \cdot k_j}{\sqrt{d_k}}, \qquad \hat\alpha = \mathrm{softmax}(\alpha), \qquad \mathrm{out}_5 = \sum_{j=1}^{5}\hat\alpha_j v_jαj​=dk​​q5​⋅kj​​,α^=softmax(α),out5​=j=1∑5​α^j​vj​

选下一个词的四种规则Four rules for choosing the next word

规则Rule 做法How 谁用Used by
贪心 Greedy 取概率最大的take the most likely word 基线；每次输出一样baseline; the same output every time
采样 Sampling 按概率随机抽draw at random by probability 聊天机器人chatbots
Top-k 采样Top-k sampling 只留最大的 k 个，重新归一化后抽keep the k largest, renormalise, then draw GPT-2，k = 40
Beam search 同时保留 B 个半成品，最后选最好的keep B partial outputs, pick the best at the end GPT-3 评测，B = 4GPT-3 evaluation, B = 4

  • 采样的做法：把概率按顺序排在 [0, 1) 上，切分点是累积和；抽 u，落在哪一段就选哪个。例：mat 0.463、floor 0.281、sofa 0.170、roof 0.063、moon 0.023，累积 0.463、0.744、0.914…，u = 0.57 → floor。

    How sampling works: lay the probabilities in order along [0, 1), with cut points at the cumulative sums; draw u and pick whichever segment it lands in. Example: mat 0.463, floor 0.281, sofa 0.170, roof 0.063, moon 0.023, cumulative 0.463, 0.744, 0.914…; u = 0.57 → floor.

  • Top-k = 切尾巴 + 拉伸：k = 3 时保留 mat、floor、sofa（和 0.914），各除以 0.914 → 0.506、0.307、0.186。为什么要切：GPT-2 有 5 万个词，尾巴里每个都很小，但加起来不小；抽到一次怪词，后面的文本都会跟着错。k = 1 就是贪心，k = V 就是普通采样。

    Top-k = cut the tail + stretch: with k = 3 keep mat, floor, sofa (sum 0.914) and divide each by 0.914 → 0.506, 0.307, 0.186. Why cut: GPT-2 has 50,000 words; each one in the tail is tiny but together they are not, and one strange word drawn derails all the text after it. k = 1 is greedy, k = V is plain sampling.

  • 例题（cup of …）：tea 0.35、coffee 0.25、water 0.20、milk 0.12、soup 0.05、sugar 0.03。k = 3 → 和 0.80 → 0.4375、0.3125、0.25；u = 0.70 落在 [0.4375, 0.75) → coffee；soup 在 k = 3 时概率 0，k = 6 时 0.05。

    Exercise (cup of …): tea 0.35, coffee 0.25, water 0.20, milk 0.12, soup 0.05, sugar 0.03. k = 3 → sum 0.80 → 0.4375, 0.3125, 0.25; u = 0.70 falls in [0.4375, 0.75) → coffee; soup has probability 0 at k = 3 and 0.05 at k = 6.

  • 温度、top-k、top-p 都是在抽取前重塑概率。选词本身是很便宜的一步。

    Temperature, top-k and top-p all reshape the probabilities before drawing. Choosing the word itself is a very cheap step.

Beam search 与长度惩罚Beam search and the length penalty

  • 贪心一旦选了就回不去；一个好的开头可能导致差的整体。Beam search 每轮把所有未完成的 beam 都扩展一个词，按累积概率（乘积）排序，只留前 B 个；遇到 EOS 的答案放到完成列表里。

    Greedy cannot undo a choice, and a good start can lead to a worse whole. Beam search extends every unfinished beam by one word each round, ranks them by cumulative probability (the product), keeps the top B, and moves answers that reach EOS to a finished list.

  • 例（B = 2，法国首都）：第 1 步 Paris 0.40、The 0.35 保留；第 2 步 The capital 0.315 反而排第一（贪心永远找不到）。最后 S =「Paris . EOS」P = 0.180、L =「The capital is Paris . EOS」P = 0.152。

    Example (B = 2, capital of France): step 1 keeps Paris 0.40 and The 0.35; at step 2 The capital 0.315 comes first (greedy could never find it). Finally S = "Paris . EOS" with P = 0.180 and L = "The capital is Paris . EOS" with P = 0.152.

  • 为什么要长度惩罚：ln P 是每个 token 一个负数相加，即使很有把握也要扣（ln 0.9 = −0.105），所以用总和永远偏向短答案；完全除以长度又太偏向长答案。所以除以长度的 α 次方，α 在 0（总和）到 1（平均）之间，GPT-3 用 α = 0.6（来自 T5）。分数高的胜出。

    Why a length penalty: ln P adds one negative number per token, a deduction even for confident tokens (ln 0.9 = −0.105), so the plain sum always favours short answers, while dividing fully by length favours long ones too much. So divide by length to the power α, with α between 0 (sum) and 1 (average); GPT-3 uses α = 0.6 (from T5). The higher score wins.

  • 法国例子：α = 0 时 S 胜（−1.715 vs −1.885）；α = 0.6 时 L 胜（−1.443 vs −1.310）；α = 1 时 L 大胜。

    France example: at α = 0 S wins (−1.715 vs −1.885); at α = 0.6 L wins (−1.443 vs −1.310); at α = 1 L wins by a lot.

  • 天空颜色例子：S =「Blue . EOS」P = 0.243（ln = −1.415），L = 六个 token P = 0.166（ln = −1.796）。α = 0.6：lp(3) = 1.188、lp(6) = 1.439，分数 −1.191 vs −1.248 → S 胜；α = 1：−1.061 vs −0.980 → L 胜。长度惩罚帮助长答案，但不保证长答案赢。贪心输出「Blue.」——只有当弱的开头带来更好的整体时，beam search 才和贪心不同。

    Sky-colour example: S = "Blue . EOS" with P = 0.243 (ln = −1.415), L = six tokens with P = 0.166 (ln = −1.796). At α = 0.6: lp(3) = 1.188, lp(6) = 1.439, scores −1.191 vs −1.248 → S wins; at α = 1: −1.061 vs −0.980 → L wins. The length penalty helps long answers but does not guarantee they win. Greedy outputs "Blue." — beam search differs from greedy only when a weaker start leads to a better whole.

score(Y)=ln⁡P(Y)lp(∣Y∣),lp(∣Y∣)=(5+∣Y∣6)α,α=0.6\mathrm{score}(Y) = \frac{\ln P(Y)}{\mathrm{lp}(|Y|)}, \qquad \mathrm{lp}(|Y|) = \Bigl(\frac{5 + |Y|}{6}\Bigr)^{\alpha}, \qquad \alpha = 0.6score(Y)=lp(∣Y∣)lnP(Y)​,lp(∣Y∣)=(65+∣Y∣​)α,α=0.6
对比Comparison GPT-2 GPT-3（评测）GPT-3 (evaluation)
选词规则Decoding rule top-k 采样，k = 40top-k sampling, k = 40 beam search，B = 4，α = 0.6
目标Goal 多样、自然的文本diverse, natural text 最可能的单一答案the single most likely answer

Quiz 10 答案与关键步骤Quiz 10 answers and key steps

点开看答案与步骤 · Show answers and steps

  • Q1 显存预算下最多几层（d = 512、V = 20,000、1024 个学出来的位置、各线性层有偏置、嵌入绑定、每个参数存值 + 梯度 + Adam 两个矩 = 4 × 4 = 16 字节、预算 2 GiB）：

    Q1 the most layers within a memory budget (d = 512, V = 20,000, 1024 learned positions, biases in every linear layer, tied embeddings, each parameter stores value + gradient + two Adam moments = 4 × 4 = 16 bytes, budget 2 GiB):

    • 每块：注意力 4 × (512² + 512) = 1,050,624；FFN (512 × 2048 + 2048) + (2048 × 512 + 512) = 2,099,712；两个 LN 2 × 1024 = 2,048；合计 3,152,384（= 12d² + 13d）。

      Per block: attention 4 × (512² + 512) = 1,050,624; FFN (512 × 2048 + 2048) + (2048 × 512 + 512) = 2,099,712; two LNs 2 × 1024 = 2,048; total 3,152,384 (= 12d² + 13d).

    • 固定部分：词嵌入 20,000 × 512 = 10,240,000；位置 1024 × 512 = 524,288；最后 LN 1,024；共 10,765,312。

      Fixed part: word embeddings 20,000 × 512 = 10,240,000; positions 1024 × 512 = 524,288; final LN 1,024; 10,765,312 in all.

    • 预算可容纳参数 2³¹/16 = 134,217,728。(134,217,728 − 10,765,312)/3,152,384 ≈ 39.2 → L = 39。

      The budget holds 2³¹/16 = 134,217,728 parameters. (134,217,728 − 10,765,312)/3,152,384 ≈ 39.2 → L = 39.

    • 验证：L = 39 共 133,708,288 个参数 × 16 B ≈ 1.9924 GiB ≤ 2；L = 40 约 2.0394 GiB > 2。

      Check: L = 39 gives 133,708,288 parameters × 16 B ≈ 1.9924 GiB ≤ 2; L = 40 gives about 2.0394 GiB > 2.

  • Q2 上下文变长后的 batch（N = 8×10⁷、12 层、d = 512，原来 64 条 × 512，现在长度 2048，每步算力不超过原来）：

    Q2 the batch after lengthening the context (N = 8×10⁷, 12 layers, d = 512; previously 64 sequences × 512, now length 2048, with per-step compute no higher than before):

    • 每 token 前向 = 2N + 注意力 12 × 4nd。n = 512：1.6×10⁸ + 12,582,912 ≈ 1.726×10⁸；训练 × 3。原预算 = 32,768 token × 3 × 1.726×10⁸ ≈ 1.697×10¹³。

      Forward per token = 2N + attention 12 × 4nd. At n = 512: 1.6×10⁸ + 12,582,912 ≈ 1.726×10⁸; training is × 3. Old budget = 32,768 tokens × 3 × 1.726×10⁸ ≈ 1.697×10¹³.

    • n = 2048：注意力 12 × 4 × 2048 × 512 = 50,331,648，每 token 训练 3 × 2.103×10⁸ ≈ 6.31×10⁸，每条序列 ≈ 1.292×10¹²。

      At n = 2048: attention 12 × 4 × 2048 × 512 = 50,331,648, training per token 3 × 2.103×10⁸ ≈ 6.31×10⁸, per sequence ≈ 1.292×10¹².

    • 1.697×10¹³ / 1.292×10¹² ≈ 13.1 → 13 条，26,624 token，约 1.68×10¹³ FLOPs；14 条就超预算。上下文变 4 倍，可用 token 数却变少了，因为注意力分数的代价随 n 增长。

      1.697×10¹³ / 1.292×10¹² ≈ 13.1 → 13 sequences, 26,624 tokens, about 1.68×10¹³ FLOPs; 14 would exceed the budget. A context 4× longer leaves fewer usable tokens, because the cost of attention scores grows with n.

  • Q3 top-k 采样（z = (ln 9, ln 3, ln 6, ln 2, 0, ln 4)，k = 3，u = 0.72）：

    Q3 top-k sampling (z = (ln 9, ln 3, ln 6, ln 2, 0, ln 4), k = 3, u = 0.72):

    • e^z = (9, 3, 6, 2, 1, 4)，概率正比于它们。前 3 名 A(9)、C(6)、F(4)，和 19。

      e^z = (9, 3, 6, 2, 1, 4), and the probabilities are proportional to these. The top 3 are A(9), C(6), F(4), summing to 19.

    • 重新归一化：(9/19, 0, 6/19, 0, 0, 4/19)。

      Renormalised: (9/19, 0, 6/19, 0, 0, 4/19).

    • 按概率从大到小排区间：A [0, 0.474)、C [0.474, 0.789)、F [0.789, 1)。u = 0.72 落在 C → 抽到 C。

      Lay out intervals from most to least likely: A [0, 0.474), C [0.474, 0.789), F [0.789, 1). u = 0.72 falls in C → the sample is C.

  • Q4 两种答案打分规则（A: (2/5, 5/8)；B: (3/5, 5/6, q)）：

    Q4 two answer-scoring rules (A: (2/5, 5/8); B: (3/5, 5/6, q)):

    • 联合概率：A = 1/4，B = q/2。规则 1 选 A ⇔ q < 1/2。

      Joint probability: A = 1/4, B = q/2. Rule 1 picks A ⇔ q < 1/2.

    • 平均 log 概率：A = ln(1/4)/2 = −ln 2，B = ln(q/2)/3。规则 2 选 B ⇔ ln(q/2) > −3 ln 2 ⇔ q > 1/4。

      Average log-probability: A = ln(1/4)/2 = −ln 2, B = ln(q/2)/3. Rule 2 picks B ⇔ ln(q/2) > −3 ln 2 ⇔ q > 1/4.

    • 答案 1/4 < q < 1/2。q = 3/8 时：A 平均交叉熵 0.693、PPL 2；B 平均交叉熵 −ln(3/16)/3 ≈ 0.558、PPL ≈ 1.75。联合概率偏向短答案，平均 log 概率去掉了这个偏差。

      Answer: 1/4 < q < 1/2. At q = 3/8: A has average cross-entropy 0.693 and PPL 2; B has −ln(3/16)/3 ≈ 0.558 and PPL ≈ 1.75. Joint probability favours short answers; the average log-probability removes that bias.

  • Q5 一步 Adam 更新共享偏置（β₁ = 0.9、β₂ = 0.95、η = 0.2）：

    Q5 one Adam step on a shared bias (β₁ = 0.9, β₂ = 0.95, η = 0.2):

    • 位置 1：logits (ln 2, 0, 0) → p = (1/2, 1/4, 1/4)，目标 B，p − y = (1/2, −3/4, 1/4)。

      Position 1: logits (ln 2, 0, 0) → p = (1/2, 1/4, 1/4), target B, p − y = (1/2, −3/4, 1/4).

    • 位置 2：logits (0, ln 2, 0) → p = (1/4, 1/2, 1/4)，目标 C，p − y = (1/4, 1/2, −3/4)。

      Position 2: logits (0, ln 2, 0) → p = (1/4, 1/2, 1/4), target C, p − y = (1/4, 1/2, −3/4).

    • 平均梯度 g = (3/8, −1/8, −1/4)。

      Average gradient g = (3/8, −1/8, −1/4).

    • m₁ = 0.1g，v₁ = 0.05g²；偏差修正后 m̂ = g，v̂ = g²；更新 = −η·g/∣g∣ = −0.2·sign(g)。

      m₁ = 0.1g, v₁ = 0.05g²; after bias correction m̂ = g and v̂ = g²; the update is −η·g/∣g∣ = −0.2·sign(g).

    • b′ = (−0.2, 0.2, 0.2)。

      b′ = (−0.2, 0.2, 0.2).

  • Q6 beam search（宽 2；P(A) = 0.55、P(B) = 0.45；A 后 EOS 0.6、X 0.4；B 后 Y 0.8、EOS 0.2；BY 后 EOS 0.9、Z 0.1）：

    Q6 beam search (width 2; P(A) = 0.55, P(B) = 0.45; after A: EOS 0.6, X 0.4; after B: Y 0.8, EOS 0.2; after BY: EOS 0.9, Z 0.1):

    • 第 1 轮：A 0.55、B 0.45。

      Round 1: A 0.55, B 0.45.

    • 第 2 轮：候选 A EOS 0.33、A X 0.22、B Y 0.36、B EOS 0.09 → 留 BY 0.36、A EOS 0.33（已完成，但仍占一个位置）。

      Round 2: candidates A EOS 0.33, A X 0.22, B Y 0.36, B EOS 0.09 → keep BY 0.36 and A EOS 0.33 (finished, but it still occupies a slot).

    • 第 3 轮：扩展 BY 得 BY EOS 0.324、BY Z 0.036；和 A EOS 一起比 → 留 A EOS 0.33、BY EOS 0.324，都完成，停止。

      Round 3: extending BY gives BY EOS 0.324 and BY Z 0.036; compared together with A EOS → keep A EOS 0.33 and BY EOS 0.324, both finished, so stop.

    • 打分（α = 0.6，长度含 EOS）：A EOS：ln 0.33/(7/6)^0.6 ≈ −1.1087/1.0969 ≈ −1.011；BY EOS：ln 0.324/(8/6)^0.6 ≈ −1.1270/1.1884 ≈ −0.948。

      Scores (α = 0.6, length including EOS): A EOS: ln 0.33/(7/6)^0.6 ≈ −1.1087/1.0969 ≈ −1.011; BY EOS: ln 0.324/(8/6)^0.6 ≈ −1.1270/1.1884 ≈ −0.948.

    • 分数高的是 BY，显示 BY；贪心：A（0.55）→ EOS（0.6），输出 A。两者不同：贪心只看当前一步，beam search + 长度惩罚看整体。

      BY scores higher, so BY is shown; greedy goes A (0.55) → EOS (0.6) and outputs A. They differ because greedy looks only at the current step, while beam search with a length penalty looks at the whole answer.

L10 易错点L10 common mistakes

  • prefill 只算 1 次全词表 softmax，训练同样 1000 个 token 要算 1000 次。

    Prefill computes the full-vocabulary softmax once; training on the same 1000 tokens computes it 1000 times.

  • KV 缓存只存 K、V，不存 Q；也不存 logits 和概率。

    The KV cache stores only K and V, not Q; nor logits or probabilities.

  • top-k 一定要重新归一化（除以保留部分的和）；k = 1 是贪心，k = V 是普通采样。

    Top-k must renormalise (divide by the kept mass); k = 1 is greedy, k = V is plain sampling.

  • 采样区间是左闭右开，按题目要求的顺序排（通常按概率从大到小）。

    Sampling intervals are closed on the left and open on the right, ordered as the question says (usually from most to least likely).

  • beam search 中已完成的序列不再扩展但仍占位置；最后按长度惩罚后的分数选，不是按概率。

    In beam search, finished sequences are no longer extended but still occupy slots; the final choice uses the length-penalised score, not the probability.

  • 长度惩罚里的分数是负数，「分数高」是绝对值小的那个。

    Length-penalised scores are negative; the "higher" score is the one with the smaller absolute value.

How to revise with these notes: (1) read each lecture's "what this lecture is about" and "common mistakes" first, to get the big picture; (2) cover the answers to each lecture's worked examples and quizzes and redo them yourself, looking at the steps only if stuck; (3) finally memorise the checklist below and the formula sheet. Nothing can be looked up in a closed-book exam, so you must be able to write all of this from memory.

必须会写的推导和证明Derivations and proofs you must be able to write

  • 有限函数类 PAC：Hoeffding + 并集界 + 三段拆分，得 n = (2/ε²)log(2∣F∣/δ)（L2）。

    PAC for a finite function class: Hoeffding + union bound + three-part split, giving n = (2/ε²)log(2∣F∣/δ) (L2).

  • VC 维：阈值 1、区间 2、矩形 4、二维线性 3，都要写「能打散 k 个」和「不能打散 k + 1 个」（L2）。

    VC dimension: thresholds 1, intervals 2, rectangles 4, 2-D linear 3; each needs both "can shatter k points" and "cannot shatter k + 1" (L2).

  • 负梯度是下降最快方向：Taylor + Cauchy–Schwarz（L3）。

    The negative gradient is the steepest descent direction: Taylor + Cauchy–Schwarz (L3).

  • sigmoid、softmax、SiLU 的导数（作业 1）。

    Derivatives of sigmoid, softmax and SiLU (Assignment 1).

  • BCE 和 softmax 交叉熵对 logit 的梯度都是 p − y（L5）。

    The gradients of BCE and softmax cross-entropy with respect to the logits are both p − y (L5).

  • XOR 不是线性可分（四个不等式矛盾），两层可以做到（L6）。

    XOR is not linearly separable (the four inequalities contradict each other), but two layers can do it (L6).

  • Score-function 恒等式、baseline 不改变梯度、均值使平方误差最小（作业 1）。

    The score-function identity, a baseline not changing the gradient, and the mean minimising squared error (Assignment 1).

  • 一层 Transformer = 12d²；临界长度 n∗ = 6d（L7）。

    One Transformer layer = 12d²; critical length n∗ = 6d (L7).

必须会答的简答题（一句话答案）Short-answer questions (one-sentence answers)

  • 传统 ML vs DL？——人工特征 vs 端到端学特征。

    Traditional ML vs DL? — Hand-crafted features vs features learned end to end.

  • 过拟合和欠拟合怎么办？——先加大模型压偏差，再正则化压方差。

    What to do about over- and underfitting? — First enlarge the model to cut bias, then regularise to cut variance.

  • 分类为什么不用 L2 损失？——sigmoid 两端梯度消失，交叉熵把 p(1 − p) 约掉。

    Why not L2 loss for classification? — Sigmoid gradients vanish at both ends; cross-entropy cancels the p(1 − p).

  • 为什么要非线性激活？——线性叠线性还是线性，做不了 XOR。

    Why non-linear activations? — Linear on linear is still linear and cannot do XOR.

  • 为什么随机初始化？——打破对称，否则同层神经元永远一样。

    Why random initialisation? — To break symmetry; otherwise neurons in a layer stay identical forever.

  • SGD 比 GD 好在哪？——便宜，而且噪声帮它离开局部最小和鞍点。

    Why SGD over GD? — It is cheap, and its noise helps it leave local minima and saddle points.

  • 动量 / RMSprop / Adam 各解决什么？——振荡 / 各方向尺度不同 / 两者都解决。

    What do momentum / RMSprop / Adam fix? — Oscillation / different scales across directions / both.

  • 为什么除以 √d_k？——让分数方差为 1，softmax 不过尖。

    Why divide by √d_k? — It keeps the score variance at 1 so the softmax is not too peaked.

  • Transformer 为什么比 RNN 好？——并行、路径短。

    Why are Transformers better than RNNs? — Parallelism and short paths.

  • MoE 为什么替换 FFN？——FFN 占每层参数的 2/3。

    Why does MoE replace the FFN? — The FFN holds 2/3 of each layer's parameters.

  • BERT 为什么用 80/10/10？——微调时没有 [MASK]，要让每个 token 都学好表示。

    Why does BERT use 80/10/10? — There is no [MASK] in fine-tuning, so every token must learn a good representation.

  • 为什么要 warm-up？——Adam 早期估计不稳、第一步每个坐标都移动 η、Post-LN 早期梯度大。

    Why warm-up? — Adam's early estimates are unstable, its first step moves every coordinate by η, and Post-LN has large early gradients.

  • GPT-1/2/3 变了什么？——架构和损失几乎不变，变的是数据、规模和用法。

    What changed across GPT-1/2/3? — Architecture and loss barely changed; data, scale and usage did.

  • prefill vs decode？——并行处理整个提示 vs 一次一个词。

    Prefill vs decode? — Processing the whole prompt in parallel vs one word at a time.

  • 为什么不缓存 Q？——Q 只在当前步用一次。

    Why not cache Q? — Q is used only once, at the current step.

常用数值（闭卷手算要用）Handy numbers for hand calculation

  • e ≈ 2.718，e² ≈ 7.389，e⁴ ≈ 54.60，e⁻¹ ≈ 0.368。

  • ln 2 ≈ 0.693，ln 3 ≈ 1.099，ln 4 ≈ 1.386，ln 8 ≈ 2.079，ln 10 ≈ 2.303。

  • σ(0) = 0.5，σ(1) ≈ 0.731，σ(2) ≈ 0.881，σ′(0) = 0.25。

  • 2¹⁰ = 1024，2²⁰ = 1,048,576，2³⁰ = 1,073,741,824（1 GiB）。

  • 一天 86,400 秒，一年约 3.15 × 10⁷ 秒，1 PF-day = 8.64 × 10¹⁹ FLOPs。

    A day is 86,400 s, a year about 3.15 × 10⁷ s, and 1 PF-day = 8.64 × 10¹⁹ FLOPs.

  • fp32 = 4 字节，fp16/bf16 = 2 字节，fp64 = 8 字节。

    fp32 = 4 bytes, fp16/bf16 = 2 bytes, fp64 = 8 bytes.

CS5242 / FORMULA AND KEY-RESULT SHEET

公式与结论速查

Formula and Key-Result Sheet

考前一页纸：左边是概念，右边是必须能默写的公式或结论。各公式的 TeX 版已在对应讲次里。

One page before the exam: concepts on the left, the formula or result you must be able to write from memory on the right. The TeX version of each formula is in the corresponding lecture.

概念Concept 必须记住的What to remember 讲Lecture
测试误差分解Test-error decomposition 测试误差 = 训练误差 + 泛化误差test error = training error + generalisation error L2
有限函数类 PACPAC for a finite class 要 n ≈ (2/ε²)·log(2∣F∣/δ) 个样本；类越大要的数据越多needs n ≈ (2/ε²)·log(2∣F∣/δ) samples; a bigger class needs more data L2
VC 维VC dimension 能打散的最大点数；d 维线性分类器（带偏置）为 d + 1，二维轴对齐矩形为 4the largest number of points that can be shattered; d + 1 for a d-dimensional linear classifier with bias, 4 for 2-D axis-aligned rectangles L2
梯度下降Gradient descent x ← x − η∇f(x)；负梯度 = 局部下降最快方向x ← x − η∇f(x); the negative gradient is the locally steepest descent L3
学习率与收敛（f = ½θ²）Learning rate and convergence (f = ½θ²) θ ← (1−α)θ；α = 1 一步到位，0 < α < 2 收敛，α = 2 振荡，α > 2 发散θ ← (1−α)θ; α = 1 converges in one step, 0 < α < 2 converges, α = 2 oscillates, α > 2 diverges L3
GD / SGD / mini-batch 用全部 / 1 个 / b 个样本的平均梯度average gradient over all / 1 / b samples L3, L6
线性回归梯度Linear-regression gradient 单样本 (wᵀx − y)x；批量 (1/m)Xᵀ(Xw − y)；解析解 (XᵀX)⁻¹Xᵀyone sample (wᵀx − y)x; batch (1/m)Xᵀ(Xw − y); closed form (XᵀX)⁻¹Xᵀy L4
链式法则Chain rule (g(h(x)))' = g'(h(x))·h'(x)；反向传播 = 从输出往回反复用它backprop = applying it repeatedly from the output backwards L4, L5
数参数Counting parameters 每层权重 = 输入数 × 输出数，偏置 = 输出数；3-4-2 网络共 20 + 6 = 26per layer weights = inputs × outputs, biases = outputs; a 3-4-2 network has 20 + 6 = 26 L5
Sigmoid σ(z) = 1/(1+e⁻ᶻ)，σ' = σ(1−σ)；两端梯度消失gradients vanish at both ends L5, L6
二元交叉熵Binary cross-entropy −[y log p + (1−y) log(1−p)]；对 z 的梯度 p − y；= 最小化负对数似然gradient w.r.t. z is p − y; = minimising negative log-likelihood L5
Softmax + 交叉熵Softmax + cross-entropy pᵢ = e^{zᵢ}/Σe^{zₖ}；损失 −Σyᵢ log pᵢ；梯度 pᵢ − yᵢ；∂pᵢ/∂zⱼ = pᵢ(δᵢⱼ − pⱼ)loss −Σyᵢ log pᵢ; gradient pᵢ − yᵢ L5
多标签 vs 多类单标签Multi-label vs single-label 多标签：每类一个 sigmoid；单标签：一个 softmaxmulti-label: one sigmoid per class; single-label: one softmax L5
偏差与方差Bias and variance 欠拟合 = 高偏差，过拟合 = 高方差；先用大模型压偏差，再正则化压方差underfitting = high bias, overfitting = high variance; a bigger model first for bias, then regularisation for variance L5, L6
数据划分Data splits 训练学参数、验证调超参、测试只报告；标准化统计量只用训练集train learns parameters, validation tunes hyperparameters, test only reports; normalisation statistics from the training set only L5
显存（普通 GD，fp32）Memory (plain GD, fp32) 参数 + 梯度 + 激活 + 输入；300B 参数、每步 1 样本 ≈ 3601 GBparameters + gradients + activations + inputs; 300B parameters, 1 sample per step ≈ 3601 GB L5
显存（Adam，fp32）Memory (Adam, fp32) 参数 + 梯度 + 一阶矩 + 二阶矩 = 4 倍参数；300B ≈ 4800 GBparameters + gradients + first + second moments = 4× parameters; 300B ≈ 4800 GB L6
为什么要非线性激活Why non-linear activations 线性层叠加仍是线性；XOR 不是线性可分stacked linear layers are still linear; XOR is not linearly separable L6
ReLU / SiLU max(0, z)，负半轴梯度 0（dying ReLU）；SiLU = zσ(z)，导数 σ(z)[1 + z(1−σ(z))]max(0, z), zero gradient for z < 0 (dying ReLU); SiLU = zσ(z), derivative σ(z)[1 + z(1−σ(z))] L6, A1
动量Momentum v ← βv + g，w ← w − αv；振荡抵消、同向加速oscillations cancel, consistent directions accelerate L6
RMSprop s ← βs + (1−β)g²，w ← w − αg/(√s + ε)；平衡各方向步长equalises step sizes across directions L6
Adam 一阶矩 β₁ = 0.9，二阶矩 β₂ = 0.999，偏差修正除以 (1 − βᵗ)first moment β₁ = 0.9, second moment β₂ = 0.999, bias correction divides by (1 − βᵗ) L6
初始化Initialisation 不能全相同（对称）；Xavier 标准差 √(2/(fan_in+fan_out))，He 标准差 √(2/fan_in)not all equal (symmetry); Xavier std √(2/(fan_in+fan_out)), He std √(2/fan_in) L6
权重衰减Weight decay J + (α/2)‖W‖²，梯度加 αw；AdamW 把衰减和 Adam 更新分开the gradient gains αw; AdamW separates decay from the Adam update L6
其他正则化Other regularisation 早停、数据增强、注入噪声early stopping, data augmentation, noise injection L6
Score function ∇E[g] = E[g·∇log p]；减常数 baseline 不改变梯度subtracting a constant baseline does not change the gradient A1
均值性质Property of the mean E‖X − a‖² = E‖X − μ‖² + ‖a − μ‖²，最小值在 a = μminimised at a = μ A1
缩放点积注意力Scaled dot-product attention softmax(QKᵀ/√d_k + M)V；掩码 M 在 softmax 之前把未来位置设为 −∞；除以 √d_k 使分数方差为 1the mask M sets future positions to −∞ before the softmax; dividing by √d_k gives scores variance 1 L7
一层 Transformer 的参数Parameters of one Transformer layer 注意力 4d² + FFN 8d² = 12d²（带偏置和两个 LN 是 12d² + 13d）；多头不增加参数attention 4d² + FFN 8d² = 12d² (12d² + 13d with biases and two LNs); multiple heads add no parameters L7, L9
整个 GPT 的参数Parameters of a whole GPT N = Vd + n_ctx·d + L(12d² + 13d) + 2d；嵌入绑定只算一次；大模型 N ≈ 12Ld²tied embeddings count once; for large models N ≈ 12Ld² L9
算力Compute 前向 2N/token，训练 6N/token，总计 6ND；注意力分数另加 4nd/token/层，临界长度 n∗ = 6d；1 PF-day = 8.64×10¹⁹ FLOPs；MFU = 所需 FLOPs ÷ (时间 × GPU 数 × 峰值)forward 2N per token, training 6N per token, 6ND in total; attention scores add 4nd per token per layer, critical length n∗ = 6d; MFU = FLOPs needed ÷ (time × GPUs × peak) L7, L9
MoE y = Σ gᵢEᵢ；∂y/∂zⱼ = gⱼ(Eⱼ − y)；Noisy top-k；Importance 和 Load 两个均衡损失，CV² = 方差/均值²two balancing losses, Importance and Load, with CV² = variance/mean² L7
位置编码Positional encoding 正余弦 PE(pos, 2i) = sin(pos/10000^(2i/d))，没有参数；BERT/GPT 用学出来的表，有 n_ctx·d 个参数sinusoidal, no parameters; BERT/GPT use a learned table with n_ctx·d parameters L7, L8
BERT 预训练BERT pre-training 选 15%：80% [MASK]、10% 随机词、10% 不变，都预测原词，只对选中位置平均；L = L_MLM + L_NSPchoose 15%: 80% [MASK], 10% random word, 10% unchanged, all predict the original word, averaged over selected positions only L8
BERT 微调BERT fine-tuning 分类：[CLS] 接 H×K；QA：S、E 两个向量，损失 −ln p_start − ln p_end，解码要求 i ≤ j；NER：每个 token 接 H×Kclassification: H×K on [CLS]; QA: vectors S and E, loss −ln p_start − ln p_end, decoding requires i ≤ j; NER: H×K on every token L8
warm-up 先线性升到峰值再衰减；原因是 Adam 早期的均值/方差估计不稳，且 Post-LN 早期梯度大；Adam 第一步每个坐标移动 ηrise linearly to the peak, then decay; because Adam's early mean/variance estimates are unstable and Post-LN has large early gradients; Adam's first step moves every coordinate by η L8, L9
困惑度Perplexity PPL = e^(平均损失)；bit = nat/ln 2；初始损失 ≈ ln V；不同分词器用 bits/byte 比较PPL = e^(average loss); bit = nat/ln 2; initial loss ≈ ln V; compare tokenisers with bits/byte L9
推理前向次数与 KV 缓存Forward passes and KV cache 每轮 = 输出词数 + 1；prefill 并行、decode 串行；prefill 只算 1 次 softmax；缓存 K、V，不缓存 Q；BERT 一次前向per turn = output words + 1; prefill parallel, decode sequential; prefill does one softmax; cache K and V, not Q; BERT takes one forward pass L9, L10
解码规则Decoding rules 贪心 = top-1；top-k 截断后重新归一化；beam search 分数 ln P(Y)/((5 + ∣Y∣)/6)^α，α = 0.6；多选题用平均每 token log 概率greedy = top-1; top-k renormalises after truncation; beam score ln P(Y)/((5 + ∣Y∣)/6)^α with α = 0.6; multiple choice uses average per-token log-probability L9, L10
三代 GPTThree GPT generations 117M / 1.5B / 175B；微调 / 零样本 / 少样本；Post-LN → Pre-LN；GPT-3 用 Adam(0.9, 0.95)、裁剪 1.0、lr 0.6×10⁻⁴、batch 3.2M tokenfine-tuning / zero-shot / few-shot; GPT-3 uses Adam(0.9, 0.95), clipping 1.0, lr 0.6×10⁻⁴, batch 3.2M tokens L9

CS5242 / SELF-TEST QUESTIONS AND ANSWERS

自测题与答案

Self-Test Questions and Answers

13 道小题，前面偏计算、后面偏概念。先自己做，再看答案。

13 short questions: calculations first, concepts later. Try them yourself before reading the answers.

  1. [L3 梯度下降] f(θ) = ½θ²，θ⁽⁰⁾ = 8，学习率 α = 0.5。走 3 步后 θ = ? 若 α = 2.5 会怎样？

    [L3 gradient descent] f(θ) = ½θ², θ⁽⁰⁾ = 8, learning rate α = 0.5. What is θ after 3 steps? What happens with α = 2.5?

    看答案 · Answer

    θ ← (1 − 0.5)θ，三步后 8 × 0.5³ = 1。α = 2.5 时 ∣1 − α∣ = 1.5 > 1，符号交替且越来越大，发散。

    θ ← (1 − 0.5)θ, so after three steps 8 × 0.5³ = 1. With α = 2.5, ∣1 − α∣ = 1.5 > 1: the sign alternates and the size grows, so it diverges.

  2. [L4 线性回归] w = (1, 2)，样本 x = (1, 1)，y = 2，损失 ½(wᵀx − y)²，α = 0.1。求预测、梯度和更新后的 w。

    [L4 linear regression] w = (1, 2), sample x = (1, 1), y = 2, loss ½(wᵀx − y)², α = 0.1. Find the prediction, the gradient and the updated w.

    看答案 · Answer

    预测 1 + 2 = 3，误差 3 − 2 = 1，梯度 = 1 × (1, 1) = (1, 1)，更新后 w = (1, 2) − 0.1 × (1, 1) = (0.9, 1.9)。

    Prediction 1 + 2 = 3, error 3 − 2 = 1, gradient 1 × (1, 1) = (1, 1), updated w = (1, 2) − 0.1 × (1, 1) = (0.9, 1.9).

  3. [L5 数参数] 10 个输入 → 5 个隐藏单元 → 3 个输出的 MLP，共有多少个可学参数？

    [L5 counting parameters] How many learnable parameters does an MLP with 10 inputs → 5 hidden units → 3 outputs have?

    看答案 · Answer

    权重 10×5 + 5×3 = 65，偏置 5 + 3 = 8，共 73 个。

    Weights 10×5 + 5×3 = 65, biases 5 + 3 = 8, 73 in total.

  4. [L5 Sigmoid] σ(0) 和 σ'(0) 各是多少？为什么说 z = 10 时会梯度消失？

    [L5 sigmoid] What are σ(0) and σ'(0)? Why does the gradient vanish at z = 10?

    看答案 · Answer

    σ(0) = 0.5，σ'(0) = 0.5 × 0.5 = 0.25（这是 sigmoid 导数的最大值）。z = 10 时 σ ≈ 0.99995，σ' ≈ 4.5 × 10⁻⁵，反向传播时乘上它梯度几乎为 0。

    σ(0) = 0.5, σ'(0) = 0.5 × 0.5 = 0.25 (the largest value of the sigmoid's derivative). At z = 10, σ ≈ 0.99995 and σ' ≈ 4.5 × 10⁻⁵, so multiplying by it in backprop leaves almost no gradient.

  5. [L5 二元交叉熵] 真实标签 y = 1，模型输出 p = 0.8。损失是多少？对 logit z 的梯度是多少？

    [L5 binary cross-entropy] True label y = 1, model output p = 0.8. What is the loss, and the gradient with respect to the logit z?

    看答案 · Answer

    损失 −ln 0.8 ≈ 0.223；梯度 p − y = 0.8 − 1 = −0.2（负号表示应增大 z）。

    Loss −ln 0.8 ≈ 0.223; gradient p − y = 0.8 − 1 = −0.2 (the minus sign means z should increase).

  6. [L5 Softmax] logit z = (0, ln 3)，真实类别是第 2 类。求概率 p、交叉熵损失和对 z 的梯度。

    [L5 softmax] Logits z = (0, ln 3), true class 2. Find p, the cross-entropy loss and the gradient with respect to z.

    看答案 · Answer

    e⁰ = 1，e^(ln 3) = 3，p = (1/4, 3/4)。损失 −ln(3/4) ≈ 0.288。梯度 p − y = (0.25, −0.25)。

    e⁰ = 1, e^(ln 3) = 3, p = (1/4, 3/4). Loss −ln(3/4) ≈ 0.288. Gradient p − y = (0.25, −0.25).

  7. [L5 调参] 多项式次数 1 / 2 / 9 的训练误差为 1.2 / 0.5 / 0.1，验证误差为 1.3 / 0.3 / 0.4。选哪个？哪个过拟合、哪个欠拟合？

    [L5 model selection] Degrees 1 / 2 / 9 have training errors 1.2 / 0.5 / 0.1 and validation errors 1.3 / 0.3 / 0.4. Which do you choose? Which overfits and which underfits?

    看答案 · Answer

    选次数 2（验证误差最小）。次数 9 训练误差最小但验证误差变大，是过拟合；次数 1 两个误差都大，是欠拟合。

    Choose degree 2 (lowest validation error). Degree 9 has the lowest training error but a higher validation error — overfitting; degree 1 has both errors high — underfitting.

  8. [L5 分类损失] 用 sigmoid 输出做分类时，为什么不用 L2 损失而用交叉熵？

    [L5 classification loss] With a sigmoid output, why use cross-entropy rather than L2 loss?

    看答案 · Answer

    L2 损失的梯度里带一个 σ'(z)，z 很大或很小时它≈ 0，就算预测错得很离谱也学不动（梯度消失）。交叉熵对 z 的梯度恰好是 p − y，错得越多梯度越大；它还等价于最大化对数似然。

    The L2 gradient contains a factor σ'(z), which is ≈ 0 when z is very large or very small, so even a badly wrong prediction cannot learn (vanishing gradient). Cross-entropy's gradient with respect to z is exactly p − y, larger the more wrong it is; it is also equivalent to maximising the log-likelihood.

  9. [L6 非线性] 为什么没有隐藏层（或只用线性激活）的网络学不会 XOR？

    [L6 non-linearity] Why can a network with no hidden layer (or only linear activations) not learn XOR?

    看答案 · Answer

    线性层叠加仍是线性函数，只能画一条直线做分界；XOR 的 (0,1)(1,0) 和 (0,0)(1,1) 在对角上，任何一条直线都分不开。需要带非线性激活的隐藏层。

    Stacked linear layers are still a linear function and can only draw one straight boundary; XOR's (0,1), (1,0) and (0,0), (1,1) sit on opposite diagonals, and no straight line separates them. A hidden layer with a non-linear activation is needed.

  10. [L6 Adam] β₁ = 0.9，m₀ = 0，第一步梯度 g₁ = 0.5。m₁ 和修正后的 m̂₁ 各是多少？为什么要修正？

    [L6 Adam] β₁ = 0.9, m₀ = 0, first gradient g₁ = 0.5. What are m₁ and the corrected m̂₁? Why correct at all?

    看答案 · Answer

    m₁ = 0.9 × 0 + 0.1 × 0.5 = 0.05；m̂₁ = 0.05/(1 − 0.9) = 0.5。因为 m 从 0 开始，初期历史不足会严重偏小；修正后第一步恰好等于当前梯度，t 大时修正自动变成 1。

    m₁ = 0.9 × 0 + 0.1 × 0.5 = 0.05; m̂₁ = 0.05/(1 − 0.9) = 0.5. Because m starts at 0, it is badly biased low while history is short; after correction the first step equals the current gradient, and for large t the correction factor becomes 1.

  11. [L6 显存] 一个 7B（70 亿）参数的模型用 Adam、fp32 训练，忽略激活和输入，至少要多少显存？

    [L6 memory] How much memory, at least, does training a 7B-parameter model with Adam in fp32 need, ignoring activations and inputs?

    看答案 · Answer

    每个参数 4 字节，7B × 4 = 28 GB；参数、梯度、一阶矩、二阶矩各一份，共 4 × 28 = 112 GB。

    4 bytes per parameter, 7B × 4 = 28 GB; one copy each of parameters, gradients, first and second moments: 4 × 28 = 112 GB.

  12. [L6 初始化] 把所有权重都初始化成 0，会发生什么？

    [L6 initialisation] What happens if every weight is initialised to 0?

    看答案 · Answer

    同一层所有神经元输出一样、梯度一样、更新也一样，永远保持相同（对称性无法打破），整层等于一个神经元；全 0 时有些层的梯度甚至全为 0。所以要随机初始化。

    Every neuron in a layer has the same output, gradient and update, so they stay identical forever (symmetry is never broken) and the layer acts as one neuron; with all zeros some layers' gradients are even entirely 0. Hence random initialisation.

  13. [L2 VC 维] 二维平面上带偏置的线性分类器 VC 维是多少？为什么 4 个点打散不了？

    [L2 VC dimension] What is the VC dimension of a linear classifier with bias in the plane? Why can 4 points not be shattered?

    看答案 · Answer

    VC 维 = 3（= d + 1）。三个不共线的点 8 种标注都能实现；4 个点时，若没有点在另三点的三角形内，把对角两点标 + 、另一对对角标 −，两条连线相交，交点必须同时为 + 和 −，矛盾；若有点在三角形内，把它标 + 、三个顶点标 − 也做不到。

    VC dimension = 3 (= d + 1). Three non-collinear points can realise all 8 labellings. With 4 points: if none lies inside the triangle of the other three, label one diagonal pair + and the other −; the two diagonals cross, and the crossing point would have to be both + and −, a contradiction. If one point lies inside the triangle, labelling it + and the three corners − is also impossible.

后半程自测题（L7–L10）Second-Half Self-Test (L7–L10)

  1. [L7] 单头注意力，d_k = 4，某位置对 3 个 token 的 q·k = (2, 0, 2)，不加掩码，求注意力权重。如果这是因果解码器的第 2 个位置呢？

    [L7] Single-head attention, d_k = 4; one position's q·k with 3 tokens is (2, 0, 2). Without a mask, find the attention weights. What if this is position 2 of a causal decoder?

    看答案 · Answer

    缩放后 (1, 0, 1)，softmax = (e, 1, e)/(2e + 1) ≈ (0.422, 0.155, 0.422)。因果第 2 位：(1, 0, −∞) → (e, 1, 0)/(e + 1) ≈ (0.731, 0.269, 0)。

    Scaled: (1, 0, 1); softmax = (e, 1, e)/(2e + 1) ≈ (0.422, 0.155, 0.422). Causal position 2: (1, 0, −∞) → (e, 1, 0)/(e + 1) ≈ (0.731, 0.269, 0).

  2. [L7] 仅解码器模型：d = 768、12 层、V = 50,000、嵌入绑定、正余弦位置编码、忽略偏置和 LN，求参数总数。

    [L7] Decoder-only model: d = 768, 12 layers, V = 50,000, tied embeddings, sinusoidal positions, ignoring biases and LN. Find the total parameter count.

    看答案 · Answer

    每层 12 × 768² = 7,077,888，12 层 84,934,656；嵌入 50,000 × 768 = 38,400,000；合计 123,334,656 ≈ 123M。

    Per layer 12 × 768² = 7,077,888, 12 layers 84,934,656; embeddings 50,000 × 768 = 38,400,000; total 123,334,656 ≈ 123M.

  3. [L7] 两个专家输出 E = (4, 0)，门控 g = softmax(z) = (1/2, 1/2)，求 y 和 ∂y/∂z。

    [L7] Two experts output E = (4, 0) and the gate is g = softmax(z) = (1/2, 1/2). Find y and ∂y/∂z.

    看答案 · Answer

    y = (4 + 0)/2 = 2；∂y/∂z₁ = g₁(E₁ − y) = 0.5 × 2 = 1，∂y/∂z₂ = 0.5 × (0 − 2) = −1。

    y = (4 + 0)/2 = 2; ∂y/∂z₁ = g₁(E₁ − y) = 0.5 × 2 = 1, ∂y/∂z₂ = 0.5 × (0 − 2) = −1.

  4. [L8] 选中 2 个位置：一个换成 [MASK]，真词概率 0.5；一个换成随机词 dog，真词 cat 的概率 0.25、dog 的概率 0.6。B 是真下一句，P(IsNext) = 0.9。求总损失。

    [L8] Two positions are selected: one becomes [MASK] with true-word probability 0.5; the other becomes the random word dog, with P(cat, the true word) = 0.25 and P(dog) = 0.6. B is the true next sentence, P(IsNext) = 0.9. Find the total loss.

    看答案 · Answer

    MLM = (ln 2 + ln 4)/2 = 1.5 ln 2 ≈ 1.040（第二个位置目标是 cat，不是 dog）；NSP = −ln 0.9 ≈ 0.105；总损失 ≈ 1.145。

    MLM = (ln 2 + ln 4)/2 = 1.5 ln 2 ≈ 1.040 (the second position's target is cat, not dog); NSP = −ln 0.9 ≈ 0.105; total loss ≈ 1.145.

  5. [L9] N = 10⁹ 的模型训练 2 × 10¹¹ 个 token，要多少 FLOPs？合多少 PF-day？

    [L9] Training a model with N = 10⁹ on 2 × 10¹¹ tokens: how many FLOPs, and how many PF-days?

    看答案 · Answer

    6ND = 6 × 10⁹ × 2 × 10¹¹ = 1.2 × 10²¹ FLOPs；÷ 8.64 × 10¹⁹ ≈ 13.9 PF-day。

    6ND = 6 × 10⁹ × 2 × 10¹¹ = 1.2 × 10²¹ FLOPs; ÷ 8.64 × 10¹⁹ ≈ 13.9 PF-days.

  6. [L9] 3 个位置正确词概率 1/2、1/2、1/8，求平均损失和困惑度。

    [L9] Correct-word probabilities at 3 positions are 1/2, 1/2, 1/8. Find the average loss and the perplexity.

    看答案 · Answer

    总损失 ln 2 + ln 2 + 3 ln 2 = 5 ln 2，平均 (5/3) ln 2 ≈ 1.155；PPL = 2^(5/3) ≈ 3.17。

    Total loss ln 2 + ln 2 + 3 ln 2 = 5 ln 2, average (5/3) ln 2 ≈ 1.155; PPL = 2^(5/3) ≈ 3.17.

  7. [L10] 输入 50 个 token、输出 20 个 token（不含 EOS），前向几次？第二轮再输入 10 个、输出 5 个，两轮共几次？

    [L10] 50 input tokens and 20 output tokens (excluding EOS): how many forward passes? A second turn with 10 inputs and 5 outputs: how many for both turns?

    看答案 · Answer

    第一轮 20 + 1 = 21 次；第二轮 5 + 1 = 6 次；共 27 次。输入长度不影响次数，只占一次 prefill。

    Turn 1: 20 + 1 = 21; turn 2: 5 + 1 = 6; 27 in total. Input length does not change the count; it takes just one prefill.

  8. [L10] 概率 (0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1)，做 top-2，分布是多少？按概率从大到小排区间，u = 0.6 抽到哪个？

    [L10] Probabilities (0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1) with top-2: what is the distribution? With intervals ordered from most to least likely, which token does u = 0.6 pick?

    看答案 · Answer

    保留 0.4、0.3，和 0.7 → (0.571, 0.429, 0, 0)。区间 [0, 0.571) 和 [0.571, 1)，u = 0.6 → 第二个 token。

    Keep 0.4 and 0.3, sum 0.7 → (0.571, 0.429, 0, 0). Intervals [0, 0.571) and [0.571, 1); u = 0.6 → the second token.

  9. [L10] 两个完整答案：S 长 2（含 EOS）、P = 0.3；L 长 5、P = 0.2。α = 0.6 时 beam search 选哪个？

    [L10] Two finished answers: S of length 2 (including EOS) with P = 0.3; L of length 5 with P = 0.2. Which does beam search choose with α = 0.6?

    看答案 · Answer

    lp(2) = (7/6)^0.6 ≈ 1.097，S 分数 = ln 0.3/1.097 ≈ −1.098；lp(5) = (10/6)^0.6 ≈ 1.359，L 分数 = ln 0.2/1.359 ≈ −1.185。S 分数更高，选 S。

    lp(2) = (7/6)^0.6 ≈ 1.097, S scores ln 0.3/1.097 ≈ −1.098; lp(5) = (10/6)^0.6 ≈ 1.359, L scores ln 0.2/1.359 ≈ −1.185. S scores higher, so S is chosen.
